     1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1646:   const groups = list
     1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1649: 
     1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1651:     return {
     1652:       regime: "neutral",
     1653:       repeatRate: 0,
     1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
>>>  1655:       samples: groups.length,
     1656:     };
     1657:   }
     1658: 
     1659:   let repeats = 0;
     1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1662:   }
     1663: 
     1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1666: 
     1667:   let regime = "neutral";
     1668: 
     1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1670:     regime = "repeat";
     1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1672:     regime = "spread";
     1673:   }
     1674: 
     1675:   return {
     1676:     regime,
     1677:     repeatRate,
     1678:     uniqueRate,
>>>  1679:     samples: groups.length,
     1680:   };
     1681: }

     1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1646:   const groups = list
     1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1649: 
     1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1651:     return {
     1652:       regime: "neutral",
     1653:       repeatRate: 0,
     1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
>>>  1655:       samples: groups.length,
     1656:     };
     1657:   }
     1658: 
     1659:   let repeats = 0;
     1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1662:   }
     1663: 
     1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1666: 
     1667:   let regime = "neutral";
     1668: 
     1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1670:     regime = "repeat";
     1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1672:     regime = "spread";
     1673:   }
     1674: 
     1675:   return {
     1676:     regime,
     1677:     repeatRate,
     1678:     uniqueRate,
>>>  1679:     samples: groups.length,
     1680:   };
     1681: }

     2839: function incMap(map, key, value = 1) {
     2840:   const k = Number(key);
     2841:   if (!Number.isFinite(k) || k < 1 || k > TOP3_GROUPS_K) return;
     2842:   map.set(k, Number(map.get(k) || 0) + Number(value || 0));
     2843: }

     1644: function detectRegimeFromComparableSequence(seqRecent) {
     1645:   const list = Array.isArray(seqRecent) ? seqRecent : [];
     1646:   const groups = list
     1647:     .map((x) => Number(x?.grupo))
     1648:     .filter((g) => Number.isFinite(g) && g >= 1 && g <= 25);
     1649: 
     1650:   if (groups.length <= 1) {
     1651:     return {
     1652:       regime: "neutral",
     1653:       repeatRate: 0,
     1654:       uniqueRate: groups.length ? 1 : 0,
>>>  1655:       samples: groups.length,
     1656:     };
     1657:   }
     1658: 
     1659:   let repeats = 0;
     1660:   for (let i = 1; i < groups.length; i += 1) {
     1661:     if (groups[i] === groups[i - 1]) repeats += 1;
     1662:   }
     1663: 
     1664:   const repeatRate = repeats / Math.max(1, groups.length - 1);
     1665:   const uniqueRate = new Set(groups).size / Math.max(1, groups.length);
     1666: 
     1667:   let regime = "neutral";
     1668: 
     1669:   if (repeatRate >= 0.34 || uniqueRate <= 0.5) {
     1670:     regime = "repeat";
     1671:   } else if (repeatRate === 0 && uniqueRate >= 0.84) {
     1672:     regime = "spread";
     1673:   }
     1674: 
     1675:   return {
     1676:     regime,
     1677:     repeatRate,
     1678:     uniqueRate,
>>>  1679:     samples: groups.length,
     1680:   };
     1681: }
